# 0. Mathematical verdict

NOT SOLVED. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. # 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.

The following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.

New joint report870832ac79589e74 gives a complete arbitrary-body actual H3 U identity, using the actual maximizer at the right child's input. Both H2 primitive reserves and every physical/auxiliary normal are retained. The natural U-only child join is refuted by the fixed interval[-1,1], children3/2 and bridge9/4, with ratio575/512 at trailing d1. Higher-depth propagation needs actual remainder transfer, not just child inequality signs. New joint reporta0925e826bbc6304 covers every positive first inner-reversing level whose initial H_J ends in the core, with full-word score<4/(5D); all starts R<=v(c-1)/(c-2) meet this gate. The exterior-source complement remains open. A coarse constant gradient bound, together with a proved pre-cap terminal and original curvature, is a new sufficient interface distinct from the false exact amplitude lemma. These are UNVERIFIED sources. The earlier gradient-projection simplex universality candidate is undergoing independent audit; it is not yet an accepted fact.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.

Kink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.

Multi-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Joint ec8a052084a6ce2e has now been FULL-read. Its complete H4 recombination retains every right-child term, and excludes a particular additive physical/auxiliary sibling pairing when the canonical initial mixture and primitive rows are frozen. The denominator gap forces positive local payment, which is negative on a common-physical-gradient formal direction. This is a certificate obstruction, not a legal endpoint counterexample.

Joint dcdf25918e7e817c has now been FULL-read. A specified all-row barycentric pairing overpays the radius of an explicit legal whole-word triangle history. A fixed sampled-hull reference with pure quadratic-product contraction also fails on that same history. The true objective still passes 1/D, with its closest polar minimum accounted for. These scoped failures do not justify further monotone increases of the same normal weights.

The current source-column route separately tests a fixed pi=sqrt(q) certificate. No result has excluded a full dual with pi strictly below sqrt(q). The odd scalar family and independent audit remain draft results; they do not imply a full-simplex endpoint theorem.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: the actual initial maximizer and physical terminal must appear with one globally consistent polynomial identity. Redistributing the initial normal requires recomputing the remainder, not appending only positive local normals. All actual right-child energies and both ordered auxiliary normal orientations must remain.

Full-dual route: every original interpolation multiplier must be nonnegative, the function-value flow must cancel exactly, and the complete quadratic must be PSD. The initial coefficient q is independent of the terminal square parameter pi until proved otherwise. A constant-gradient restriction is only a necessary affine Gram test; a core-product equality is only another necessary test.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not call a formal PSD obstruction an admissible objective adversary. Do not identify the canonical initial mixture with the whole certificate cone. Do not infer all-depth closure from accepted H3. Do not impose q=pi^2 without a proved necessity. Do not replace all original rows by scalar restrictions or assume an unknown feasible matrix. Keep the whole polar minimum set and original initial-radius normalization.

# 4. Missing bridges

One concrete route must obtain a sufficient all-body H4 identity with changed initial attachments, and only then consider a quantified reusable rule. A different route must derive the full unsaturated-terminal coefficient interface and test a literal new PSD family. A complete uniform full-depth bound or uniform all-word lower witness remains necessary for the original Goal.

Do not export a symmetric-Huber radial argument to asymmetric or scaled curvatures without proving the changed envelope. Do not optimize child start points independently. Do not reset enclosing-m parameters at subtrees. Do not identify a three-sector endpoint certificate with full independent-gradient PSD.

Every benchmark comparison uses ALL integer splits; the UPPER bound B_n/D_m<47/50 cannot establish a lower witness's gain by reversal. No finite scan, exact recurrence alone, exponent-only result, conditional matrix inequality or restricted-family bound completes the original target.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

The positive bridge is an actual selector V(h)<=W_n(h) for every finite h>=0 with B_n V(h)>=1-o(1) uniformly at every sufficiently large n. One static smooth convex function, original chronology, complete minimum and true radius are required. This retains theorem-level strength.

The negative bridge is an explicit nonnegative original-row certificate (or other full-function argument) on actual unbounded words with a fixed benchmark improvement. The exact full-space Schur condition and its all-depth coefficients must be proved, not posed as an unknown SDP. Accepted931 supplies only the final scalar comparison once such a bound exists.

The four current interfaces are decisive tests of different mechanisms for those bridges. Exact obstructions should name their entire parameter domains and what remains outside. Neither a legal construction without quantitative gain nor a relaxation counterexample without interpolation may be described as a solved bridge.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.

The following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Kink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.

Multi-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.

This missing inequality is of theorem-level strength.

Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.
- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

NOT SOLVED. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 proves the all-split benchmark bound and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. A FULL-class upper W(H_m)<=17/(16D_m) would yield a fixed separation; that inequality remains of theorem-level strength and is not assumed. Accepted d8b59c7d92425121 represents each finite-word full supremum by affine-height envelopes over at most n+2 polytope vertices, with true initial radius and an orthogonal height vector. It is a structural equality, not a quantitative rate.

The quantitative proof skeleton still requires either an explicit original-space nonnegative interpolation certificate with a paid PSD remainder, or a uniform legal adversary for every word. The current explicit certificate architecture and the scalar/geometric diagnostic routes are independent. None closes the original target.

|---|---|---|---|
|Benchmark comparison|ACCEPTED|931b9653ce0f7201, at its exact scope|Full-class upper hypothesis|
|Full-function finite-word representation|ACCEPTED|d8b59c7d92425121|Uniform quantitative polytope estimate|
|Literal H2 primitive reserve|JOINT UNVERIFIED|Explicit eight-row certificate, report 9fcef03a7d29785f|H3 and higher actual auxiliary-remainder payment|
|Odd outward-kink diagnostic|JOINT UNVERIFIED|All starts in the absorbing-core region, report d34bc8275bb55379|Intermediate core reversal and return regimes|

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.

The following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.

Full joint reportb2a4bfc15411d7af derives all triangle projection guards and an exact side-child energy identity. The scalar distance tube fails on a legal H1, and the scalar small-radius capture fails on a legal full H_m with finite orthogonal radius; neither example beats1/D. The entire first-leaf upper-to-lower pattern with a same-lower-side suffix is uniformly below1/D because its inverse leaf and rotation cost are paid. Root-bridge switches and a coupled folded state for repeated side changes are genuinely new interfaces. All these claims remain UNVERIFIED.

Corrected joint report3211401a8cc8819d excludes the entire prescribed original-root switch pattern. Its exact folded correction is generally positive and not a uniform small perturbation of the unchanged same-side surrogate; a legal H1 supplies a fixed positive correction but does not violate the true objective bound. The new interface is the exact rank-one factorization of all opposite-face interpolation rows and a corresponding fixed two-ray gradient body. Neither has yet supplied the missing total original-radius payment. All new claims are UNVERIFIED.

The projection-simplex universality candidate has a complete independent native audit and the actual H3 U candidate has a separate main textual bridge/normal audit. Their joint self-contained theorem would give finite-word full-supremum representation, an arbitrary-body seven-step U base block, and the exact full-class W7 profile for that block. It is selected for one scoped gate, still UNVERIFIED until service acceptance and fact persistence. No all-depth quantitative theorem or original minimax conclusion is included.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.

Kink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.

Multi-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

An actual clipped full-word counterexample refutes replacing both curvatures by K in the radial plateau. The large surviving radius is paid for by its small terminal curvature; it does not improve the objective.

Full report7662d3077f76bf26 gives all original triangle guards and a finite algebraic formula for the entire initial-state profile. The complete core and every fixed face have score at most1/D_m, but no uniform comparison is proved for changing faces. The inradius-only one-step radial envelope is false at a legal original bridge. A reachable strict vertex switch has positive charge with zero transverse release; phase-dependent face constants/transverse squares cannot make all switches nonpositive. The actual sum of switch charges is still unbounded relative to initial reserve. These are UNVERIFIED mathematical statements; neither family controls full W.

Latest full-prefix development, joint UNVERIFIED: reportd134cc9e6dbd9769 proves the adaptive one-anchor selector on all zero-height legal prefixes in arbitrary dimension and on max_i delta_i<=||x0||^2/(12D sqrt(m)), using a fully bounded suffix-product sum e_m<6sqrt(m). It gives exact true full-prefix values on the root-sign quadratic and Huber, and a genuine two-dimensional passing family. The original all-positive-height correlation cover remains unproved and unrefuted. Height differences alter the original normal conditions; they cannot be erased while retaining the projection argument. The complete signed height telescope and a true proximal height function are potential new interfaces, not accepted results.

Latest independent-common-column result762b936882b2b6bb is joint UNVERIFIED. It derives the entire original polynomial, all optimum moments, first-row total/clock and last-row penultimate-clock. Positive initial incoming load and an independent clock-zero column still fail under the two explicit laws: the endpoint/tail law fails the first clock, while terminal concentration plus a penultimate metric atom repairs that clock but forces a remote last-query negative multiplier. The remaining problem is simultaneous all-row matching, not a missing PSD factor alone. A terminal pool with clock exactly2 and a jointly chosen late metric fraction is a concrete new candidate, not a verified assertion.

Latest joint draft36b125191170ba08 proves an exact scalar-relaxation loss1/(S_j+1), nearly2/D_j, with a single actual fixed-edge trajectory attaining the radius history but not the separately maximized terminal objective. Its U_d=d Psi(x/d) storage has an exact normal-mixing one-step lower difference, positive for h<=3/2, and pays the actual strict-switch H3 test with ratio<7/64 for all trailing d>=1. Universal complete-child U_d payment is still unproved; long-step pointwise monotonicity is false. This motivates keeping actual child and nonlocal projection-normal reserves. It retains physical input caps d_j*w and curvature-dependent output radii;the resulting energy1/(q*d_m^2) overlaps the true target above q_cut. The low-q root-return case is jointly rederived. The entire result remains UNVERIFIED and covers neither an outward increase of curvature within a ray nor arbitrary scalar or multidimensional functions. Full joint report227313d14c0c37bf establishes that the V=2 law passes both complete endpoint screens but fails incoming last-column capacity:the invariant P_ll<1/3 cannot carry2alpha_l>19/10. Its transport/variance cut excludes the full stated terminal-concentrated,source-heavy metric family with the clock-zero source/end common column. A common column supported at the final two queries with nonzero clock changes exactly that restriction,but every moment,initial charge and original row must be recomputed. All these new claims and the proposed successor remain UNVERIFIED.

Accepted factd8b59c7d92425121 now closes a FULL finite-word representation:W_n(h)=W_aff_n(h) for arbitrary finite n,h>=0,where affine-height polytope Moreau envelopes have<=n+2 vertices,unique minimum0 and a nonzero initial point orthogonal to the height vector. Its strict-source lift preserves horizontal dynamics and the true initial radius exactly;regularization covers every original selected minimizer. This is a verified equality of suprema,not a quantitative bound. The relevant gradient is X-P_K(X-lambda),not P_K(X). A uniform quantitative estimate over EVERY represented polytope would now transfer through an accepted fact. Bounds on exposed segments or a chosen triangle still would not cover that full class.

Full joint report677a3071c5a3b021 gives the complete exposed-segment reduction with true orthogonality/radius payment and explicit finite tilt. Core starts,thin segments,no/one-crossing histories,and specified large radii pass;an actual all-m upper-wing reactivation disproves erasing the finite upper curvature boundary after a plateau visit. The whole segment profile remains open. Signed interval endpoints plus distance to[0,L] offer a distinct candidate state,but a tube without the within-interval height cannot prove the objective bound. These new claims remain UNVERIFIED.

# 2. Interface contract table

### Original full-space certificate
Input required: every original Q_ij<=0, value cancellation, nonnegative multipliers, and PSD on independent x_0,g_0,...,g_n, with the initial charge paid once. Output claimed: W_n(h)<=q. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 gives the row orientation and terminal completion. Missing verification on the actual model: explicit coefficient law with all finite and optimum signs at q<=17/(16D_m). Failure mode if ignored: a signed factor can pass PSD while proving no upper bound. Status:PARTIAL.

### Separate terminal and common directions
Input required: original conjugate coordinates, explicit probabilities, independent common column z with e^Tz=p+s, explicit actual skew divergence and paid zero-sum imbalance, all original signs. Output claimed: a potential extension of the restricted matching architecture. Available facts:1252b8823bb5a290 only for original row algebra; the displayed extension in research/INDEPENDENT_COMMON_SQUARE_ADVISORY_20261003.md is not accepted. Missing verification: the joint full identity, global load/row moments and an actual sign-feasible law. Failure mode: treating a free common square as free transport or assuming its PSD implies interpolation signs. Status:PARTIAL.

### Signed scalar energy
Input required: one static clipped derivative with independent positive curvatures, all starts, exact thresholds and actual same-m children. Output claimed: a quantitative family objective profile or improving legal witness. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 only compares a subsequently proved bound with the benchmark. Missing verification: independent-curvature multiple crossings while retaining destination energy and caps. Failure mode: a weak curvature permits a large radius that is not a large objective. Status:PARTIAL.

### Full triangle face changes
Input required: the centered equilateral body, all initial positions and all original face guards, with the actual Euclidean denominator. Output claimed: a uniform quantitative profile or an explicit all-depth legal selector. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 supplies scalar benchmark comparison only. Missing verification: change-of-face recursion with tangential terms and simultaneous guards. Failure mode: optimizing incompatible children, discarding a transverse coordinate, or selecting a finite expression without bounding it. Status:PARTIAL.

### Quantitative conclusion in the original model
Input required: a full-class upper bound on the displayed H_m or a uniform all-word lower bound with the exact benchmark. Output claimed: disproof by fixed separation, or the requested limit. Available facts:931b9653ce0f7201 closes the negative scalar comparison after the missing hypothesis; b98bb807d514e80d fixes the positive normalization. Missing verification: the quantitative full-class inequality. Failure mode: restricted bodies/functions and finitely many horizons do not cover the goal. Status:SUBSTANTIAL.

# 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

- DANGEROUS: 'Full PSD and optimum signs are enough.' Original finite row signs are a separate requirement;121bbbe4b6a46fe3 isolates a first-row clock obstruction after all those screens pass.
- DANGEROUS: 'The larger curvature controls every radius.' The explicit clipped counterexample in7476ebfe7be45713 retains radius while paying small objective curvature. A correct energy statement must retain the terminal side.
- DANGEROUS: 'Every fixed face is Huber-optimal, so changing faces are too.' Actual projection maps are nonlinear across guards and tangential motion survives a bridge.
- DANGEROUS: 'Degree at most8 means the uniform triangle maximum is known.' The maximizing face history still ranges over the full original word.
- DANGEROUS: 'Simplex representation supplies a schedule-only lower witness.' The representation starts from an already chosen run, with a full polar minimum cone.
- DANGEROUS: 'A failed formal PSD direction is a convex-smooth counterexample.' All original interpolation rows still need to hold for a single static function.

Concrete anti-routes: fixing a known impossible load law while retuning only balanced skew; replacing independent curvature by its maximum in a radial lemma; dropping the last leaf; resetting child m; propagating a convex hull through a nonlinear map without justification; calling scoped family results full W bounds. These choices lose identified load-bearing constraints.

# 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge1: explicit common-direction and load matching
Target statement: for every same-m H_m above, construct actual original-row nonnegative coefficients with value cancellation and a complete independent-vector PSD factor at17/(16D_m). Prerequisites: explicit finite formulas for all weights and one globally paid initial charge. Existing facts:1252b8823bb5a290,931b9653ce0f7201 at stated scopes. Missing checks: common/terminal coupling, incoming and outgoing optimum loads, all finite row signs and objective level. Closure criterion: all original signs and the full factor hold at all m>=1536, with no feasible-matrix oracle. Independent of the diagnostic family branches.

### Bridge2: independent-curvature terminal energies
Target statement: for every positive b_plus,b_minus, positive kappa_plus,kappa_minus<=1 and real start s, establish the full same-m score <=K/(1+2KS), K=max curvatures, or construct an explicit full-word family violating this candidate with exact payoff. Prerequisites: one global clipped function and actual threshold/sign history. Existing facts: original normalization b98bb807d514e80d; benchmark comparison931b9653ce0f7201. Missing checks: all repeated returns with source/destination energy ratios and genuine tails. Closure criterion: all independent parameter ranges and starts, not an unevaluated recurrence. Independent; a success still covers only the stated family.

### Bridge3: changing-face triangle profile
Target statement: for D=conv{(1,0),(-1/2,sqrt3/2),(-1/2,-sqrt3/2)}, F_D(x)=max_(g in D)(x.g-||g||^2/2), obtain a uniform bound on sup_(x_0!=0)2F_D(Psi_m(x_0))/||x_0||^2 or an explicit all-depth witness. Prerequisites: all actual projection guards, coordinates and the true radius. Existing facts:931b9653ce0f7201 for a later comparison only. Missing checks: changing supports, compatible child states and tangential energies. Closure criterion: quantified all-m objective bound or legal selector; finite algebraic enumeration alone is insufficient. Independent; no automatic extension to arbitrary dimension.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

NOT SOLVED. The original arbitrary-schedule exact minimax profile target remains open, W=2R. Accepted1e0bf3f41457d8e4 supplies finite-word projection-simplex universality and only the actual H3 all-body endpoint base. No accepted full-depth full-class bound is present.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

FULL-read joint37660b4102b12fe7 derives the entire balanced-cap common-curvature kink family at(1+21/(4m))/D, and one precisely scoped original-root larger-tail to smaller-tail itinerary below3/(5D). Both are UNVERIFIED. The new exact signed interval, ratio-paid saturated branch and asymmetric Huber comparison replace odd symmetry. The complete original-root example with caps8 and1 shows that an a*Bmax gradient cap does not imply a pre-cap smaller-ray terminal.

The separately FULL-read source certificate report excludes two fixed source laws but not the schedule or general duals. Common-shift Gray diagnostics do not cover all initializer values. None supplies a missing scalar lemma here.

# 2. Interface contracts

One fixed common curvature kappa and knot1, with two independently fixed caps, must govern the entire original word. A complete-child capture statement must identify the actual terminal ray and its own cap. A smaller-tail terminal retains its linear energy and tail intercept. Any original-radius price after an earlier crossing must be proved; the first-crossing price cannot simply be reused for the last crossing.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not reuse balanced capture outside Bmin/Bmax>=c-2. Do not replace tail energy by gradient squared/kappa. Do not turn the root all-tail result into an arbitrary-itinerary bound. Do not reset child starts, remove physical suffixes, or import an unverified prior draft as an accepted fact.

# 4. Missing bridges

The next concrete interfaces are asymmetric complete-child capture toward the larger-cap ray and original-radius payment for every smaller-tail terminal, including repeated crossings. A whole scalar-family theorem would still be scoped; it would not finish the original full-function minimax Goal.

# 0. Mathematical verdict

NOT SOLVED. The original target remains the exact every-horizon, every-nonnegative-word limit W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1, or a rigorous full-function asymptotic separation. W=2R. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 proves the all-split benchmark bound and B_(2^m-1)/D_m<47/50. Accepted d8b59c7d92425121 represents each finite-word full supremum by affine-height envelopes over at most n+2 polytope vertices, with true initial radius and an orthogonal height vector. It is a structural equality, not a quantitative rate.

The quantitative proof skeleton still requires either an explicit original-space nonnegative interpolation certificate with a paid PSD remainder, or a uniform legal adversary for every word. The current explicit certificate architecture and the scalar/geometric diagnostic routes are independent. None closes the original target.

# 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Accepted 1252b8823bb5a290 retains every original interpolation row and the real terminal. Accepted 04219554e41d9750 gives the exact-root primitive identities, but does not justify arbitrary bridge shortening. Accepted 931b9653ce0f7201 supplies only the conditional quantitative comparison.

The following are draft results, not accepted premises. Full report 9fcef03a7d29785f derives the off-root identity V_parent=V_left+K V_right-(K-1)E+R(||g_p||^2-||g_z||^2). Its literal H2 has positive row multipliers and a path-square reserve. R changes sign at H3; child primitive positivity alone fails on an actual fixed interval, while the retained initial-maximization remainder pays that interval family. Full report d34bc8275bb55379 proves the entire absorbing-core region at (1+21/(4m))/D and refutes the unpriced all-amplitude gradient lemma even for unit Huber. Its necessary coefficient loss is only O(1/m), but does not prove a sufficient repair. Full report 5960de36af8e17d5 derives exact signed interval images, geometric complete-child capture, and original chronological prefix payment, yielding the entire exposed-segment subclass supremum 1/D. Every finite orthogonal tilt is strictly below this supremum. The result is not a reduction of general polytopes to segments.

The full free-terminal law report10965e841ab7298f is also JOINT UNVERIFIED. It establishes its literal full square factor and moment compatibility, but the canonical completion fails a tail entry. Its source/last terminal replacement is excluded by exact source cuts; all-completion feasibility of the original bracket law was open. A subsequent fixed finite LP discovery found a ten-entry tail-six dual pattern with no marginal contribution. The corresponding literal symmetric-factor identity in MAIN_EXACT_CUT_LEAD.md is a new analytical lead, not an accepted obstruction. Correlated clock-null PSD details offer a different repair mechanism from scalar reweighting.

# 2. Interface contracts

Projection U route: one fixed compact convex D containing zero, F_D=max_g(x.g-||g||^2/2), gradient P_D, U_d=max_g(2x.g-d||g||^2). Every claimed U law must hold at arbitrary actual starts and every trailing d>=1, with the real initial maximizer and all original rows. This is not the affine-height residual-gradient class.

Kink route: one fixed odd derivative sign(x) min(B,max(kappa|x|,|x|-(1-kappa))), all B>=kappa>0, true initial radius, all physical steps and the same enclosing m. Gradient capture alone is insufficient because terminal objective changes with core curvature and kink area.

Multi-face route: one fixed K and height vector, gradient X-P_K(X-lambda), with every projection normal, original terminal and actual orthogonal radius retained. A plane reduction needs a finite perpendicular tilt; independent face choices and a reset radius are not legal.

# 3. Dangerous heuristics

Do not promote unverified scalar profiles into a full W bound. Do not erase upper-wing reactivation, equate independently maximized radius and energy, omit the actual initial-maximization remainder, compound an O(1/m) loss once per level without paying the total, or reset the enclosing m in a child. A fixed-face proof is not a changing-face proof. A failed signed certificate is not an interpolable counterexample.

# 4. Missing bridges

First, pay the actual arbitrary-body U remainder through H3 and derive a reusable quantitative state instead of the false primitive-sign induction. Second, control first inner reversal and later entries/exits for the odd kink family using an absolute total loss. Third, derive a genuine two-face affine-height estimate or an actual full-word witness whose objective survives the true initial radius and finite tilt. Each is a scoped diagnostic; the full-class quantitative inequality or uniform all-word lower witness remains the central unresolved bridge.
